<script>
'use strict';
const catalog=JSON.parse(document.getElementById('catalog').textContent);
const sections=['模块用途与业务流程','列表字段及线框','新增字段及线框','编辑、详情和其他操作','后端接口与数据处理','实施顺序与验收例子'];
const escape=v=>String(v??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
let selected=catalog.find(p=>p.id===location.hash.slice(1))||catalog[0],section=1,formIndex=0;
const q=s=>document.querySelector(s),el=id=>document.getElementById(id);
function table(headers,rows,cls=''){return `<div class="scroll"><table class="${cls}"><thead><tr>${headers.map(h=>`<th scope="col">${escape(h)}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(r=>`<tr>${r.map(v=>`<td>${escape(v)}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`}
function list(items){return `<ol>${items.map(s=>`<li>${escape(s)}</li>`).join('')}</ol>`}
function nav(){el('nav').innerHTML=[...new Set(catalog.map(p=>p.group))].map(group=>`<details ${group===selected.group?'open':''}><summary>${escape(group)}</summary>${catalog.filter(p=>p.group===group).map(p=>`<button type="button" data-page="${p.id}" class="${p===selected?'current':''}">${escape(p.title)}</button>`).join('')}</details>`).join('');el('nav').querySelectorAll('[data-page]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{selected=catalog.find(p=>p.id===b.dataset.page);formIndex=0;history.replaceState(null,'','#'+selected.id);nav();render()})}
function render(){el('title').textContent=selected.title;el('purpose').innerHTML=`<p>${escape(selected.purpose)}</p><span class="badge">${escape(selected.group)}</span> <span class="badge">${escape(selected.role)}</span>`;el('tabs').innerHTML=sections.map((t,i)=>`<button type="button" data-section="${i}" class="${i===section?'selected':''}" aria-pressed="${i===section}">${i+1}. ${t}</button>`).join('');el('tabs').querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{section=Number(b.dataset.section);render()});
const p=selected,s=p.module,c=el('content');
if(section===0){c.innerHTML=`<section class="panel"><h3>本模块连续业务流程</h3>${list(s.flow)}<h3>本页面的业务规则</h3><p>${escape(p.rule)}</p><div class="note">组织资料说明“在哪里、能做什么”；资格说明“能否做”；专属产能说明“做多快”；日历说明“何时可用”。候选方案复验并发布后才形成正式计划。</div></section>`;return}
if(section===1){renderList();return}
if(section===2){renderForm();return}
if(section===3){c.innerHTML=`<section class="panel"><h3>详情应能核对的资料</h3><ul>${p.extra.map(t=>`<li>${escape(t)}</li>`).join('')}</ul><h3>编辑和其他操作</h3><p>${escape(p.actions.join('、'))}</p><p>基础资料读取当前版本后编辑；已生效的产能、工艺和正式计划保留历史，通过新版本或业务动作调整。报工及数量事实采用追加更正。具体允许状态和处理依本模块正文。</p><div class="note">无当前范围权限时隐藏写入入口；后台仍须检查权限。保存冲突保留输入，重读最新对象后比较。</div></section>`;return}
if(section===4){c.innerHTML=`<section class="panel"><h3>后台处理顺序</h3>${list(s.backend)}${table(['业务用途','方法与接口','现状','输入','输出','处理范围'],p.api.map(a=>[a.purpose,a.method+' '+a.path,a.state,a.input,a.output,a.table]))}<p>数据归属：${escape(s.tables)}</p><div class="note">“已有契约”表示接口已在唯一契约中定义，业务运行结果另记；“拟补接口”需设计确认后实施。事务、权限、时区、版本冲突和幂等均由后台处理。</div></section>`;return}
c.innerHTML=`<section class="panel"><h3>实施顺序</h3>${list(s.steps)}<h3>业务验收例子</h3>${s.tests.map((t,i)=>`<article class="panel"><h3>${i+1}. ${escape(t[0])}</h3><p>前提：${escape(t[1])}</p><p>操作：${escape(t[2])}</p><p>预期：${escape(t[3])}</p><p>核对：${escape(t[4])}。另保留对应实际页面截图。</p></article>`).join('')}<div class="note">这些是执行方法和独立预期，本图稿不记录实际验收通过。</div></section>`}
function renderList(){const p=selected;el('content').innerHTML=`<section class="panel"><div class="filtergrid">${p.filters.map((f,i)=>`<div><label for="filter-${i}">${escape(f)}</label><input id="filter-${i}" placeholder="请选择或输入"></div>`).join('')}</div><div class="toolbar"><button class="primary" id="query">查询</button><button id="clear">清空</button>${p.actions.map((a,i)=>`<button class="mock-action" data-action="${i}">${escape(a)}</button>`).join('')}</div><div class="labelflex"><label for="list-state">设计状态</label><select id="list-state"><option>有数据</option><option>暂无数据</option><option>正在读取</option><option>读取失败</option><option>无权限</option></select><button id="dictionary-toggle" aria-expanded="false">字段说明</button></div><div id="list-result" class="panel"></div><div id="dictionary" class="hidden">${table(['展示字段','例子','数据来源','业务规则'],p.columns.map(f=>[f.label,f.example,f.displaySource,f.rule]))}</div><div class="note">${escape(p.rule)}</div>${p.id==='P21'?'<h3>同一计划事实的排程图布局</h3><div class="timeline"><div>张师傅 · 焊锡</div><div><span class="bar">08:00—10:00 · 100件（含准备）</span></div><div>装配工位</div><div><span class="bar" style="margin-left:30%">后续装配 · 等待上道放行</span></div></div>':''}</section>`;
function paint(){const state=el('list-state').value;el('list-result').innerHTML=state==='有数据'?table(p.columns.map(f=>f.label),[p.columns.map(f=>f.example)],'listtable')+'<div class="toolbar"><span>共1条合成记录 · 第1页</span><button disabled>上一页</button><button disabled>下一页</button><span>每页20条（候选）</span></div>':`<div class="statecard"><h3>${escape(state)}</h3><p>${escape({'暂无数据':'没有符合条件的记录；保留筛选。可以清空条件或从资料页面新增。','正在读取':'显示读取范围并等待，不能把旧数据称为本轮查询结果。','读取失败':'保留筛选和当前页面，说明失败原因并提供重试。','无权限':'本设计状态不显示业务记录，写入操作也不可用。'}[state])}</p></div>`;q('.toolbar').querySelectorAll('.mock-action').forEach(b=>b.disabled=state==='无权限')}
el('list-state').onchange=paint;el('query').onclick=()=>{el('list-state').value='有数据';paint()};el('clear').onclick=()=>q('.filtergrid').querySelectorAll('input').forEach(i=>i.value='');el('dictionary-toggle').onclick=()=>{const hidden=el('dictionary').classList.toggle('hidden');el('dictionary-toggle').setAttribute('aria-expanded',String(!hidden))};document.querySelectorAll('.mock-action').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{section=selected.forms.length?2:3;render()});paint()}
function control(f,i){const id='field-'+i,example=String(f.example),select=f.control.includes('选择')||f.control.includes('多选'),base=`id="${id}" data-key="${escape(f.key)}" data-index="${i}"`;
if(f.control==='只读')return `<input ${base} readonly value="${escape(example)}">`;
if(f.control==='开关')return `<select ${base}><option${example==='是'?' selected':''}>是</option><option${example!=='是'?' selected':''}>否</option></select>`;
if(f.control.includes('子表')||f.control.includes('明细'))return `<div class="subtable" data-key="${escape(f.key)}"><table><thead><tr><th>关联对象或子字段</th><th>业务值</th><th>操作</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>在独立明细表中填写</td><td>见对应产品行、阶段或资源要求表单</td><td>选择、编辑</td></tr></tbody></table><button type="button" class="add-row">添加未提交行</button><div class="hint">父对象保存前在本地编辑，父对象与明细按同一事务提交。</div></div>`;
if(f.control==='结构化条件编辑')return `<div class="subtable"><label for="${id}">业务条件名称</label><input ${base} placeholder="例如配方、规格、设备能力"><label for="${id}-value">条件值</label><input id="${id}-value" placeholder="选择或输入业务值"><div class="hint">正式实现采用结构化规则编辑器，由客户端生成协议对象。</div></div>`;
if(select)return `<select ${base} ${f.control.includes('多选')?'multiple aria-description="可选择多项"':''}>${f.renderOptions.map(t=>`<option>${escape(t)}</option>`).join('')}</select>`;
if(f.control==='多行文本')return `<textarea ${base} placeholder="${escape(example)}"></textarea>`;
if(f.control.includes('日期'))return `<input ${base} type="datetime-local" value="${example.includes('2026-')?escape(example.replace(' ','T')):''}">`;
const type=f.control.includes('输入')&&(f.datatype==='整数'||f.datatype==='小数')?'number':'text',value=f.default==='空'?'':(type==='number'?(Number.isFinite(Number(f.default))?f.default:''):f.default);
return `<input ${base} type="${type}" ${type==='number'?'step="'+(f.datatype==='整数'?'1':'any')+'"':''} value="${escape(value)}" placeholder="${escape(example)}">`}
function renderForm(){const p=selected,c=el('content');if(!p.forms.length){c.innerHTML=`<section class="panel"><h3>本页面的产生方式</h3><p>${escape(p.noCreate)}</p><div class="note">通过来源业务动作产生并查询，在本页不提供普通新增表单。</div></section>`;return}
const frm=p.forms[formIndex];c.innerHTML=`<section class="panel"><div class="labelflex"><label for="form-choice">表单或业务动作</label><select id="form-choice">${p.forms.map((f,i)=>`<option value="${i}" ${i===formIndex?'selected':''}>${escape(f.title)}</option>`).join('')}</select></div><h3>${escape(frm.title)}</h3><p>入口：${escape(frm.entry)}</p><p>${escape(frm.source)}</p><div class="labelflex"><label for="form-state">设计状态</label><select id="form-state"><option>正常填写</option><option>必填错误</option><option>正在保存</option><option>保存完成</option><option>版本冲突</option>${p.id==='P24'?'<option>候选已过期</option>':''}<option>无写入权限</option></select></div>${frm.title==='维护工序阶段'?'<div class="toolbar"><label><input id="phase-context" type="checkbox" checked style="width:auto"> 当前工序允许中断（设计状态）</label></div>':''}<form id="business-form" novalidate><div class="formgrid panel">${frm.fields.map((f,i)=>`<div data-condition="${escape(f.condition)}" data-field-index="${i}" class="${f.control.includes('子表')||f.control.includes('明细')?'full':''}"><label for="field-${i}">${escape(f.label)}${f.required==='可选'?'':' <span class="required">*</span>'}</label>${control(f,i)}<div class="hint">${escape(f.required+'；'+f.source+'；'+f.rule)}</div><div class="field-error hint"></div></div>`).join('')}</div><div class="note">${escape(frm.rules)}</div><div id="form-feedback" role="status" aria-live="polite"></div><div class="actions"><button id="save" class="primary" type="submit">${escape(frm.primary)}</button><button id="cancel" type="button">取消，返回列表</button></div></form><details class="panel"><summary>输入字段与处理说明</summary>${table(['输入字段','控件与格式','必填条件','默认值','联动来源','校验反馈'],frm.fields.map(f=>[f.label,f.control+'；'+f.datatype,f.required,f.default,f.source,f.rule]))}<p>提交：${escape(frm.save)}</p><p>成功后：${escape(frm.result)}</p></details></section>`;
el('form-choice').onchange=()=>{formIndex=Number(el('form-choice').value);renderForm()};el('cancel').onclick=()=>{section=1;render()};
function value(key){return q(`[data-key="${key}"]`)?.value||''}
function conditional(){const mode=value('calculationMode'),duration=value('durationModel'),op=value('mode'),dep=value('dependencyType');document.querySelectorAll('[data-condition]').forEach(box=>{let visible=true;const t=box.dataset.condition;if(t==='hour')visible=mode!=='固定批周期';if(t==='batch')visible=mode==='固定批周期';if(t==='duration-fixed')visible=duration==='固定时长'||duration==='固定加按件';if(t==='duration-unit')visible=duration==='按件时长'||duration==='固定加按件';if(t==='operation-batch')visible=op==='批处理';if(t==='threshold')visible=dep==='数量门槛';if(t==='transfer')visible=dep==='数量门槛';if(t==='interruptible')visible=value('phaseType')==='加工'&&!!el('phase-context')?.checked;box.classList.toggle('hidden',!visible)})}
el('business-form').onchange=conditional;if(el('phase-context'))el('phase-context').onchange=conditional;document.querySelectorAll('.add-row').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{const tr=document.createElement('tr');tr.innerHTML='<td><input aria-label="新增明细对象" placeholder="选择业务对象"></td><td><input aria-label="新增明细值" placeholder="填写业务值"></td><td><button type="button">删除未提交行</button></td>';tr.querySelector('button').onclick=()=>tr.remove();b.closest('.subtable').querySelector('tbody').append(tr)});
function feedback(state){const f=el('form-feedback');el('save').disabled=['正在保存','无写入权限'].includes(state);f.className='';f.textContent='';if(state==='正常填写')return;f.className='feedback'+(['必填错误','版本冲突','候选已过期'].includes(state)?' error':'');f.textContent={'必填错误':'请补齐当前方式的必填字段，错误应定位到对应控件。其他输入保留。','正在保存':'正在保存设计状态：禁止重复提交。','保存完成':'设计状态演示：'+frm.result+'。正式实现须再次读取确认。','版本冲突':'资料已变化，保留当前输入。请重新读取最新版本并比较。','候选已过期':'候选所依据的日历、资格或执行事实已变化。发布拒绝，当前正式计划保持。','无写入权限':'当前范围没有该动作权限，保存入口不可用。'}[state]}
el('form-state').onchange=()=>feedback(el('form-state').value);el('business-form').onsubmit=e=>{e.preventDefault();const state=el('form-state').value;if(['版本冲突','候选已过期','无写入权限','正在保存'].includes(state)){feedback(state);return}let invalid=false;frm.fields.forEach((f,i)=>{const box=q(`[data-field-index="${i}"]`),input=el('field-'+i);box.querySelector('.field-error').textContent='';if(box.classList.contains('hidden')||!input||f.control==='只读')return;const required=f.required==='必填'||f.required.includes('方式必填')||f.required.includes('时必填');if(required&&!input.value.trim()){invalid=true;box.querySelector('.field-error').textContent='请填写'+f.label;box.querySelector('.field-error').classList.add('required')}});el('form-state').value=invalid?'必填错误':'保存完成';feedback(el('form-state').value)};conditional()}
window.addEventListener('hashchange',()=>{const p=catalog.find(p=>p.id===location.hash.slice(1));if(p){selected=p;formIndex=0;nav();render()}});nav();render();
</script></body></html>
